Optimization loop details:

```
def polish(a, n, stop_t, aim, rng, verbose=False):
    a = normalize(a)
    c = conv(a,a)
    sc = 2*n*c.max()  # since sum=1
    M= c.max(); avg=1/(2n-1)
    alpha = 0.15
    fail=0
    std_fail=0
    beta = 30.0
    while time.monotonic() < stop_t:
        c = conv(a,a); M=c.max(); sc=2*n*M (sum1)
        # update best outside? We'll pass best list; check inside.
        low = aim_M if aim_M < M else max(avg*1.0001, M*0.995)
        if low < avg: low=avg*1.0001
        # target
        target = M - alpha * max(0.0, M - low)
        if target <= low: target = (target+low)/2
        cand = newton_step(a,c,target)
        if cand is not None and scand < sc*(1-1e-12):
            a, c, sc, M = cand...
            alpha = min(1.0, alpha*1.05 + 0.01)
            fail=0
            std_fail=0
        else:
            fail+=1
            # try gradient
            if fail in (1,2,4,7) or alpha <0.05:
                beta = min(200, beta*1.5) maybe periodically reset
                gdir = gradient_delta(a,c,beta)
                cand = line_search(a,c,gdir)
                if cand improves: ... beta= max(20,beta/2?) #?
                else:
                    if fail>=8 and fail%10==0: perturb
            if fail > 15:
                # perturb if not near best
                if sc < best_global? maybe small
                a = normalize(a * np.exp(0.05*rng.standard_normal(n)))
                c=conv
                if sc > local_best*(1+0.05)?? We have local best from global? Keep local best a? Could restore.
                alpha = max(0.1, alpha*0.5)
                fail=0 after perturb? 
```

def newton_step(a,c,target):
 n=a.size; m=2*n-1; avg=1.0/m
 M=c.max()
 if not finite or target <= avg: return None
 if target >= M: target = M - max(1e-9, 1e-6*(M-avg))
 if target < avg: target=avg*(1+1e-12)
 d=box_project(c,target)
 e=c-d

 except: return None
 delta=sol
 delta=delta - delta.mean()
 if not finite or norm<1e-300: return None
 return line_search(a,c,delta, fast_cur_score=2*n*max(c))

Implementation of line_search details:
```
def line_search(a,c,delta,cur_score):
 n=a.size
 delta=np.asarray(delta,float); delta=delta - delta.mean()
 dnorm=np.linalg.norm(delta); anorm=np.linalg.norm(a)
 if dnorm<=1e-300 or anorm<=...: return None
 l1=np.convolve(a, delta)
 c2=np.convolve(delta, delta)
 # limits
 neg_delta = delta < -1e-15
 if neg_delta.any(): s_max = np.min(-a[neg_delta]/delta[neg_delta]) * (1-1e-12)
 else: s_max = inf
 pos_delta = delta > 1e-15
 if pos_delta.any(): s_min = -np.min(a[pos_delta]/delta[pos_delta]) * (1-1e-12)
 else: s_min = -inf
 # generate
 rels = [1e-5,3e-5,1e-4,3e-4,1e-3,3e-3,1e-2,3e-2,0.1,0.3,1,3,10,30]
 step_scale = anorm/dnorm
 cands=[]
 for t in rels:
   s=t*step_scale
   if s> s_max*(1+1e-12): continue
   cands.append(s)
   s2=0.1*t? covered
 # include raw 1.0, 0.1, 10?
 for sraw in [0.01,0.03,0.1,0.3,1,3,10]:
   if 0 < sraw < s_max*(1+1e-12): cands.append(sraw)
 # negative
 if s_min < -1e-10:
   for t in [1e-4,1e-3,3e-3,1e-2,3e-2,0.1,0.3,1,3]:
      s=-t*step_scale
      if s < s_min*(1+1e-12): continue
      cands.append(s)
   for sraw in [-0.01,-0.03,-0.1,-0.3,-1,-3]:
      if sraw > s_min*(1-1e-12): cands.append(sraw)
 # maybe include s around 0 if no positive? at least 0 no.
 # unique
 uniq=[]; cands=sorted(set?) floats. Use loop with rel tol.
 best_s=None; best_sc=cur_score
 for s in uniq:
   if s <= 0? allow negative.
   if s > s_max or s < s_min: continue
   a_s = a + s*delta
   mn = a_s.min()
   if mn < -1e-10: continue
   if mn < 0: a_s=np.maximum(a_s,0)
   # If clipped significantly, skip? clip amount <=1e-10, okay.
   cs = c + 2*s*l1 + s*s*c2
   # due clip, cs approximate; if clip >1e-8, recompute exact? For selection, if max(np.abs(a_s - (a+s*delta))) >1e-8 skip maybe.
   S = a_s.sum() # should be 1
   if S <= 1e-300: continue
   mc = cs.max()
   if mc < 0 and abs(mc)<1e-14: mc=0
   sc = 2*n*mc/(S*S)
   if sc < best_sc: best_sc=sc; best_s=s
 if best_s is None: return None
 a_new = a + best_s*delta
 if a_new.min()< -1e-10: return None
 a_new = np.maximum(a_new,0)
 a_new = a_new/a_new.sum()
 # exact score of a_new
 sc_new = fast_score(a_new)
 if sc_new < cur_score - 1e-12:
    return a_new, sc_new
 return None
```
Note: `c` passed is c(conv a) maybe exact from normalized a. If `a_s` clipped/renormalized, cs not exact. But if small clip, okay. We recompute exact sc_new for acceptance; for selection, approximate might mislead if clip large. Could if min near zero, cs invalid; skip if `a_s.min() < -1e-8`. For small negative -1e-12, ok. If s at boundary, renormalization changes shape slightly; exact score may differ. We recompute and accept only if better than cur_score. Good.

Potential: The line search may find a_s with significantly lower exact score only after clipping, but our candidate selection could miss due approximate. Since we allow small clip, okay. If direction pushes into boundary, maybe best at boundary not in interior. We could also include candidates that are clipped and renormalized but use approximate with clipped a? To handle, after line search, if best near boundary, maybe no accept. But score often optimum interior. Could add `a_clip` candidate for s slightly beyond? Not necessary.

`gradient_delta`:
```
def gradient_delta(a,c,beta=50):
 m=c.max(); if m<=0: return None
 z=beta*(c-m); w=np.exp(z - z.max()); wsum=w.sum(); if wsum==0: return None; w/=wsum
 s=hankel_vec(a,w)
 delta=-s; delta-=delta.mean()
 return delta
```
Could also use active delta:
```
def active_delta(a,c,frac=0.1):
 M=c.max(); avg=1/m; tol=max(1e-12, frac*(M-avg)); active=c >= M - tol; if active.sum()==0 active[c.argmax()]=True; w=np.zeros(m); w[active]=1/active.sum(); delta=-hankel_vec(a,w); delta-=mean; return delta
```
We can use active with different tol.

LP direction:
```
def lp_delta(a, M, time_limit):
 if linprog is None: return None
 n=a.size; m=2*n-1
 A=np.zeros((m,n))
 # fill vectorized? for j in range(n): A[j:j+n,j]=a
 for j in range(n): A[j:j+n,j]=a
 c=-np.ones(n)
 b=M*(1+1e-9)+1e-12
 try:
   res=linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=b, bounds=[(0,None)]*n, method='highs', options={'presolve':True,'time_limit':time_limit})
   if res.success:
      g=np.clip(res.x,0,None); sg=g.sum(); if sg>1e-12: g/=sg; delta=g-a; delta-=delta.mean(); if norm>...
 except: pass
 return None
```
The LP max sum g with f*g <= M. If M small, g maybe small sum <1? After normalization, okay. But if M below avg, maybe no feasible with sum1? If g raw small, after normalization its convolution with a may exceed M. The previous method used same: LP max sum under M, rescale to sum1. They argued nonincreasing? Maybe not if M small? But line search exact will decide. It's just direction.

`polish` function with global best:
```
def polish(a, stop_t, aim_score, best, rng, allow_lp=True):
 a=normalize(a); n=a.size
 c=np.convolve(a,a); sc=fast_score(a)
 local_best_a=a.copy(); local_best_sc=sc
 update_global(a,best)
 alpha=0.12; fail=0; beta=30.0; low_fail=0
 last_lp=time
 while time.monotonic() < stop_t - 1.0:
   a=normalize(a); c=np.convolve(a,a); sc=fast_score(a) # maybe just 2n cmax since sum1
   M=c.max(); avg=1/(2*n-1)
   if sc < local_best_sc: local_best...; update_global
   aim_M = (aim_score - 0.001)/(2*n) # maybe aim_score=TARGET, subtract 0.001
   if aim_M < avg: aim_M=avg*(1+1e-12)
   if M > aim_M:
      low=aim_M
   else:
      low=max(avg*(1+1e-8), M*0.998) # continue 0.2%
   # if low too close to M, min_red
   red=M-low
   if red <= 0: target=M
   else:
      target=M - alpha*red
      if target > M: target=M
      if M-target < 1e-11: target=M - max(1e-11, 1e-4*red)
   # try newton
   step=None
   if red > 1e-12:
      step=newton_step(a,c,target,sc)
   if step is not None:
      a,sc=step; alpha=min(1, alpha*1.03+0.005); fail=0; beta=30; continue
   # if no newton, gradient
   tried_grad=False
   for beta_try in [beta, 50, 150, 500]:
      if time...
      delta=gradient_delta(a,c,beta_try)
      if delta is None: continue
      step=line_search(a,c,delta,sc)
      if step:
        a,sc=step; beta=beta_try; fail=0; break
   if step: continue
   # active delta variants
   for frac in [0.05,0.2,0.5]:
      delta=active_delta(a,c,frac)
      step=line_search(...)
      if step: ...
      fail+=1
      if step: break
   if step: continue
   # LP occasionally
   if allow_lp and linprog is not None and time.monotonic() > last_lp and time.monotonic() > stop_t-30:
      last_lp=time+20? actually set now
      delta=lp_delta(a, M*(1+1e-7), time_limit=max(2, min(15, stop_t-time-30)))
      if delta is not None:
         step=line_search(a,c,delta,sc)
         if step: a,sc=step; fail=0; alpha=0.1; continue
   fail+=1
   if fail >= 8:
      # perturb small
      sigma=0.03 + 0.02*(fail//8)
      for _ in range(3):
        pert=normalize(a*np.exp(sigma*rng.standard_normal(n)))
        psc=fast_score(pert)
        if psc < local_best_sc*(1+0.03):
           a=pert; sc=psc; break
      else:
        a=local_best_a.copy(); sc=local_best_sc; c=np.convolve(a,a)
      alpha=max(0.08, alpha*0.6); fail=0
   elif fail > 30:
      # revert
      a=local_best_a.copy(); c=np.convolve(a,a); sc=local_best_sc; alpha=0.08; fail=0
 return a
```

Detailed code:
```
import numpy as np, time, math
try:
    from scipy.linalg import solve_toeplitz
except Exception:
    solve_toeplitz = None
try:
    from scipy.optimize import linprog
except Exception:
    linprog = None

`_clean_arr(a)`: nan_to_num, clip.
```
def _clean(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size==0: return np.array([1.0])
    a=np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    return np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
```

`_normalize(a)` returns float array sum1 length same (or if sum too small uniform).
```
def _normalize(a):
    a=_clean(a)
    if a.size==0: return np.array([1.0])
    s=float(a.sum())
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s<1e-300:
        return np.full(a.size, 1.0/a.size)
    return a/s
```

`_score(a)` normalized:
```
def _score(a):
    a=_clean(a)
    if a.size<1: return inf
    s=a.sum()
    if s<1e-300: return inf
    b=np.convolve(a,a)
    mb=float(b.max())
    if not math.isfinite(mb) or mb<0: mb=0
    return float(2*a.size*mb/(s*s))
```

`_update_best(a,best)`:
```
def _update_best(a,best):
    try:
      a=_normalize(a)
      if a.size<1: return
      v=evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in a])
      if math.isfinite(v) and v < best[0] - 1e-15:
        best[0]=v; best[1]=a.copy()
    except Exception: pass
```

`_resample(arr,n)`:
```
def _resample(arr,n):
  arr=_clean(arr)
  if n < 1: n=1
  if arr.size == 0: return np.full(n,1.0/n)
  if arr.size == 1: return np.full(n,1.0/n)
  s=arr.sum()
  if s<=1e-300: return np.full(n,1.0/n)
  old=np.linspace(0,1,arr.size); new=np.linspace(0,1,n)
  vals=np.interp(new, old, arr/s)
  return _normalize(vals)
```

`_box_project(c,t,S=None)` as above. Ensure returns sum S. We can pass S=sum(c) to be robust.
```
def _box_project(c,t,S=None):
    c=np.asarray(c,float)
    S = c.sum() if S is None else S
    m=c.size; avg=S/m
    if not math.isfinite(t) or t < avg: t=avg*(1+1+1e-12)? Actually if t<avg, feasible infeasible; return c? Set t=avg*(1+1e-12)
    if np.max(c) <= t*(1+1e-14): return c.copy()
    low = float(np.min(c)) - t - max(1.0, float(np.max(c)))
    high = 0.0
    # f(low) >=0, f(high) <=? maybe high=0 f<=0 if cap sum <S. If no cap sum<S? If max<=t returned. If max>t, f(0)=sum clip(c,0,t)-S <0 because at least one cap. Good.
    for _ in range(60):
      mid=(low+high)*0.5
      val=np.sum(np.clip(c-mid,0,t))
      if val < S: high=mid
      else: low=mid
    lam=(low+high)*0.5
    d=np.clip(c-lam,0,t)
    return d
```
If `max(c)>t` but val(0) may still >S? How? If c sum S and c has values above t, clip sum <S because lowered some, unless also negative none. So <. Good.
If t huge no cap returned.

`_hankel(a,e)` with e length m.
```
def _hankel(a,e):
    n=int(a.size); L=2*n-2
    h=np.convolve(a, e[::-1], mode='full')
    return h[L-n+1:L+1][::-1]
```
Check slice when n=0? no. For n=1, L=0, L-n+1=0, L+1=1.

`_autocorr(a)`:
```
def _autocorr(a):
    n=a.size
    if n==1: return a*a
    full=np.correlate(a,a,mode='full')
    r=full[n-1:n-1+n].copy()
    # small negative? autocorr positive; clip
    np.maximum(r,0,out=r)
    return r
```

`_solve_toeplitz(r,b)`:
```
def _solve_toeplitz(r,b):
    n=len(r)
    base=float(r[0]) if r[0]>0 else 1e-15
    eps=1e-12*max(1.0,base)
    for k in range(12):
        rc=r.copy(); rc[0]=base+eps
        if solve_toeplitz is not None:
            try:
                sol=solve_toeplitz(rc, rc, b)
                if np.all(np.isfinite(sol)):
                    return sol
            except Exception:
                pass
        # dense
        try:
            C=np.zeros((n,n), dtype=np.float64)
            for lag in range(n):
                val=rc[lag]
                idx=np.arange(n-lag)
                C[idx, idx+lag]=val
                if lag:
                    C[idx+lag, idx]=val
            C[np.diag_indices(n)] += eps*10
            sol=np.linalg.solve(C,b)
            if np.all(np.isfinite(sol)): return sol
        except Exception:
            pass
        eps *= 10.0
    # last try lstsq
    try:
      C=...; C[np.diag]+=eps*10; sol=np.linalg.lstsq(C,b,rcond=None)[0]; return sol
    except: return None
```
Dense building each attempt O(n^3) solve; for n512 fallback could be slow. But solve_toeplitz should work. To reduce fallback cost, set max fallback attempts 3. If too slow, time check? The solve fallback inside many iterations if solver fails could hang. We can add a time guard: pass `stop_t`? Simpler: only fallback if n<=384; else use dense with sparse? But n<=512. We can do max attempts=3. If still fail, return None. If `solve_toeplitz` fails every iter, dense 512 attempts 3 per iter too slow. But likely not.

Maybe instead of dense in every attempt, first try solve_toeplitz with increasing jitter 5 attempts; if fail, dense once with large jitter. E.g.:
```
for k in range(6): try solve_toeplitz; eps*=10
# dense once with eps=1e-8*base
```
Good.

In dense build, use `toeplitz` from scipy? `scipy.linalg.toeplitz(rc, rc)` builds C; may be faster. We can import `toeplitz` too. But manual okay. Let's import:
```
try: from scipy.linalg import solve_toeplitz, toeplitz as _toeplitz
except: _toeplitz=None
```

    rels=[1e-6,3e-6,1e-5,3e-5,1e-4,3e-4,1e-3,3e-3,1e-2,3e-2,0.1,0.3,1,3,10,30]

          if sraw >= s_min*(1-1e-12): cands.append(sraw)

    cands.sort()

       sc = 2.0*n*mc/(S*S)
       if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
           best_sc=sc; best_s=s
    if best_s is None: return None
    a_new = a + best_s*delta
    if a_new.min() < -1e-10: return None
    a_new = np.maximum(a_new,0.0)
    a_new = a_new / a_new.sum()
    sc_new = _score(a_new)
    if math.isfinite(sc_new) and sc_new < cur_score - 1e-13:
       return a_new, sc_new
    return None

`_newton_direction` maybe build δ and call line_search.
```
def _newton_step(a,c,target,cur_score):
    n=a.size; m=2*n-1; avg=1.0/m
    M=float(c.max())
    if not math.isfinite(M) or M<=0: return None
    if target > M: target=M
    if target <= avg:
       target = avg*(1+1e-12) if avg>0 else M*0.99
    red=M-target
    if red <= max(1e-14, 1e-8*M):
       return None
    d=_box_project(c,target)
    e=c-d
    # if e all zero return
    if not np.any(np.abs(e)>1e-16*M): return None
    B=_hankel(a,e)
    r=_autocorr(a)
    sol=_solve_toeplitz(r, -0.5*B)
    if sol is None: return None
    delta=sol - sol.mean()
    return _line_search(a,c,delta,cur_score)
```
The `red <= 1e-8*M` skip if too tiny. But final reductions might be 1e-8? We set min_red in polish bigger.

`_lp_delta`: optional.

```
def _lp_delta(a,M, time_limit=5.0):
    if linprog is None: return None
    n=a.size
    if n>400: return None
    m=2*n-1
    A=np.zeros((m,n), dtype=np.float64)
    for j in range(n):
        A[j:j+n,j]=a
    b_ub=np.full(m, M*(1+1e-8)+1e-12)
    cvec=-np.ones(n)
    try:
       res=linprog(cvec, A_ub=A, b_ub=b_ub, bounds=[(0.0,None)]*n, method='highs', options={'presolve':True,'time_limit':time_limit})
    except Exception:
       try:
          res=linprog(cvec, A_ub=A, b_ub=b_ub, bounds=[(0.0,None)]*n, method='highs')
       except Exception: return None
    if res is not None and getattr(res,'success',False) and res.x is not None:
       g=np.clip(res.x,0,None); g=np.nan_to_num(g,0,0,0); sg=g.sum()
       if sg>1e-14:
          g/=sg; delta=g-a; delta-=delta.mean()
          if np.linalg.norm(delta)>1e-15: return delta
    return None
```
Maybe if LP returns g with sum zero? no.

Potential issue: LP with M current max: If current c_i=M for many, constraints f*g≤M. It can choose g large on coordinates where a small. Direction likely. Good.

def _make_seeds(n, parent, best, rng, limit=12):
    seeds=[]
    x=np.linspace(0.1,0.9,n) maybe? For endpoints, 0 and1 better. Use 0,1.
    def add(a):
       a=_clean(a)
       if a.size!=n: a=_resample(a,n)
       a=_normalize(a)
       if a.size==n and a.sum()>0: seeds.append(a)
    if parent is not None:
       add(_resample(parent,n))
       # noise
       for sig in (0.05,0.15,0.3):
           add(_resample(parent,n) * np.exp(sig*rng.standard_normal(n)))
    if best is not None and best.size>=2:
       add(_resample(best,n))
    add(np.ones(n))
    # exponentials
    xm=x-0.5
    for c in (0.3,0.7,1.5,3.0,-0.3,-0.7,-1.5,-3.0):
       add(np.exp(c*xm))

       add(1.0 + 2.0*np.abs(x-0.5)**p) # center low, endpoints high? 1+0.25 center? Actually abs max at endpoints .5, min center0.
    # sinus
    for k in (1,2,3):
       for amp in (0.3,0.6,0.9):
          v=1+amp*np.sin(2*np.pi*k*x)
          add(np.clip(v,1e-3,None))
          v=1+amp*np.cos(2*np.pi*k*x)
          add(np.clip(v,1e-3,None))
    # random
    for alpha in (0.3,0.7,1.7,3.0):
       try: add(rng.dirichlet(np.full(n,alpha)))
       except: pass
    # random lognormal
    for sig in (0.3,0.8,1.5):
       add(np.exp(sig*rng.standard_normal(n)))
    # deduplicate? Evaluate scores sort. To avoid many same, we can simple limit 30.
    # score all
    scored=[]
    for a in seeds:
       if len(scored)>50: break
       s=_score(a)
       if math.isfinite(s): scored.append((s,a))
    scored.sort(key=lambda x:x[0])
    # choose top `limit` with diversity if time. Simpler top limit
    out=[a for _,a in scored[:limit]]
    return out
```

def _polish(a0, stop_t, best, rng, base_aim=1.499, allow_lp=True):
    a=_normalize(a0); n=a.size
    if n < 8 or time.monotonic() >= stop_t - 5: return a

    local_best_a=a.copy(); local_best_sc=sc; local_best_M=M

    while time.monotonic() < stop_t - 1.0:
        a=_normalize(a); c=np.convolve(a,a); sc=_score(a); M=c.max()
        if sc < local_best_sc - 1e-14:
            local_best_sc=sc; local_best_a=a.copy(); local_best_M=M
        # update global if near
        if sc < best[0] + 1e-6:
            _update_best(a,best)
        avg=1.0/(2*n-1)
        aim = base_aim
        if math.isfinite(best[0]):
            aim = min(aim, best[0] - 0.002)
        if aim < 1.0: aim = 1.0 + 1e-6
        aim_M = aim/(2.0*n)
        if M <= aim_M:
            red = 5e-5*M
        else:
            gap = M - aim_M
            red = alpha*gap
            min_red = max(1e-12, 5e-5*M)
            if red < min_red: red = min_red
            if red > M - avg*1.0: red = M - avg*1.0
            if red <= 0: red = min_red
        target = M - red
        step=None
        if target > avg and red > 1e-13:

            delta=_lp_delta(a, M, tl)

                if step is not None:

        fail += 1
        if fail == 8 or (fail > 8 and fail % 6 == 0):

```python
import numpy as np
import time
import math

try:
    from scipy.linalg import solve_toeplitz, toeplitz as _scipy_toeplitz
except Exception:
    solve_toeplitz = None
    _scipy_toeplitz = None

try:
    from scipy.optimize import linprog
except Exception:
    linprog = None

TARGET = 1.50274349
BASE_AIM = min(1.500, TARGET - 0.001)


def _clean(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    return np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)


def _normalize(a):
    a = _clean(a)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-300:
        a = np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        a = a / s
    # Keep strictly positive for stable Toeplitz solvers; effect is negligible.
    a = a + 1e-12
    a = a / a.sum()
    return a


def _score(a):
    a = _clean(a)
    if a.size < 1:
        return float("inf")
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-300:
        return float("inf")
    c = np.convolve(a, a)
    m = float(c.max()) if c.size else 0.0
    if not math.isfinite(m):
        return float("inf")
    if m < 0.0 and m > -1e-15:
        m = 0.0
    return float(2.0 * a.size * m / (s * s))


def _update_best(a, best):
    try:
        a = _normalize(a)
        if a.size < 1:
            return
        v = evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in a])
        if math.isfinite(v) and v < best[0] - 1e-15:
            best[0] = float(v)
            best[1] = a.copy()
    except Exception:
        pass


def _resample(arr, n):
    arr = _clean(arr)
    n = int(max(1, n))
    if arr.size == 0:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
    if arr.size == 1:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-300:
        arr = np.full(arr.size, 1.0 / arr.size, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        arr = arr / s
    old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, arr.size, dtype=np.float64)
    new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    vals = np.interp(new, old, arr)
    return _normalize(vals)


def _box_project(c, t):
    """Euclidean projection of c onto {0 <= x <= t, sum x = sum c}."""
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    if c.size == 0:
        return c
    S = float(c.sum())
    if not math.isfinite(S) or S <= 0.0:
        return c
    m = c.size
    avg = S / m
    if not math.isfinite(t):
        return c
    if t <= avg:
        t = avg * (1.0 + 1e-12)
    if float(c.max()) <= t * (1.0 + 1e-14):
        return c

    lo = float(c.min()) - t - max(1.0, float(c.max()))
    hi = 0.0
    for _ in range(70):
        mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        val = float(np.sum(np.clip(c - mid, 0.0, t)))
        if val < S:
            hi = mid
        else:
            lo = mid
    lam = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
    d = np.clip(c - lam, 0.0, t)
    return d


def _hankel(a, e):
    """
    Return q_i = sum_k e_k a_{k-i}, i=0..n-1.
    Used for A^T e where A maps v -> convolve(a, v).
    """
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    L = 2 * n - 2
    h = np.convolve(a, e[::-1], mode="full")
    return h[L - n + 1:L + 1][::-1]


def _autocorr(a):
    """r[l] = sum_i a_i a_{i+l}, l=0..n-1."""
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 1:
        return a * a
    full = np.correlate(a, a, mode="full")
    r = full[n - 1:n - 1 + n].copy()
    r = np.nan_to_num(r, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    np.maximum(r, 0.0, out=r)
    return r


def _solve_toeplitz(r, b):
    n = int(len(r))
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    base = float(r[0])
    if not math.isfinite(base) or base <= 0.0:
        base = 1e-15
    eps = 1e-12 * max(1.0, base)

    # Fast Stable SingleChildScrollView-style solver if available.
    for _ in range(8):
        rc = r.copy()
        rc[0] = base + eps
        if solve_toeplitz is not None:
            try:
                sol = solve_toeplitz(rc, rc, b)
                sol = np.asarray(sol, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
                if sol.size == n and np.all(np.isfinite(sol)):
                    return sol
            except Exception:
                pass
        eps *= 10.0

    # Dense fallback, only for moderate sizes.
    if n <= 400:
        try:
            rc = r.copy()
            rc[0] = base + 1e-8 * max(1.0, base)
            if _scipy_toeplitz is not None:
                C = np.asarray(_scipy_toeplitz(rc, rc), dtype=np.float64)
            else:
                C = np.zeros((n, n), dtype=np.float64)
                for lag in range(n):
                    val = rc[lag]
                    idx = np.arange(n - lag)
                    C[idx, idx + lag] = val
                    if lag:
                        C[idx + lag, idx] = val
            C[np.diag_indices(n)] += 1e-8 * max(1.0, base)
            sol = np.linalg.solve(C, b)
            if np.all(np.isfinite(sol)):
                return sol
        except Exception:
            pass

    # Last resort least squares.
    try:
        right = b
        rc = r.copy()
        rc[0] = base + 1e-7 * max(1.0, base)
        if _scipy_toeplitz is not None:
            C = np.asarray(_scipy_toeplitz(rc, rc), dtype=np.float64)
        else:
            C = np.zeros((n, n), dtype=np.float64)
            for lag in range(n):
                val = rc[lag]
                idx = np.arange(n - lag)
                C[idx, idx + lag] = val
                if lag:
                    C[idx + lag, idx] = val
        C[np.diag_indices(n)] += 1e-7 * max(1.0, base)
        sol = np.linalg.lstsq(C, right, rcond=None)[0]
        if np.all(np.isfinite(sol)):
            return sol
    except Exception:
        pass

    return None


def _line_search(a, c, delta, cur_score):
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return None
    m = 2 * n - 1
    delta = np.asarray(delta, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if delta.size != n:
        return None
    delta = np.nan_to_num(delta, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    delta = delta - float(delta.mean())
    dn = float(np.linalg.norm(delta))
    an = float(np.linalg.norm(a))
    if dn <= 1e-300 or an <= 1e-300 or not math.isfinite(dn) or not math.isfinite(an):
        return None

    l1 = np.convolve(a, delta)
    c2 = np.convolve(delta, delta)
    if l1.size != m or c2.size != m:
        return None

    tol = 1e-14
    neg = delta < -tol
    if neg.any():
        ratios = -a[neg] / delta[neg]
        s_max = float(np.min(ratios)) * (1.0 - 1e-10)
    else:
        s_max = math.inf

    pos = delta > tol
    if pos.any():
        ratios = a[pos] / delta[pos]
        s_min = -float(np.min(ratios)) * (1.0 - 1e-10)
    else:
        s_min = -math.inf

    if not math.isfinite(s_max) or s_max <= 0.0:
        # No positive step possible; still allow negative steps if available.
        pass
    if not math.isfinite(s_min):
        pass

    step_scale = an / dn
    rels = [
        1e-6, 3e-6, 1e-5, 3e-5, 1e-4, 3e-4, 1e-3,
        3e-3, 1e-2, 3e-2, 1e-1, 3e-1, 1.0, 3.0, 10.0, 30.0,
    ]
    cands = []
    for rr in rels:
        s = rr * step_scale
        if 0.0 < s <= s_max * (1.0 + 1e-12):
            cands.append(float(s))
    for sraw in [1e-4, 3e-4, 1e-3, 3e-3, 1e-2, 3e-2, 0.1, 0.3, 1.0, 3.0, 10.0, 30.0]:
        if 0.0 < sraw <= s_max * (1.0 + 1e-12):
            cands.append(float(sraw))

    if s_min < -1e-12:
        for rr in rels:
            s = -rr * step_scale
            if s >= s_min * (1.0 - 1e-12):
                cands.append(float(s))
        for sraw in [-1e-4, -3e-4, -1e-3, -3e-3, -0.01, -0.03, -0.1, -0.3, -1.0, -3.0, -10.0]:
            if sraw >= s_min * (1.0 - 1e-12):
                cands.append(float(sraw))

    cands.sort()
    uniq = []
    for s in cands:
        if not math.isfinite(s):
            continue
        if not uniq or abs(s - uniq[-1]) > max(1e-10, 1e-4 * max(abs(s), abs(uniq[-1]))):
            uniq.append(float(s))

    best_s = None
    best_sc = cur_score
    for s in uniq:
        if s > s_max or s < s_min:
            continue
        a_s = a + s * delta
        if a_s.size < 1:
            continue
        if float(a_s.min()) < -1e-10:
            continue
        cs = c + 2.0 * s * l1 + (s * s) * c2
        S = float(a_s.sum())
        if not math.isfinite(S) or S <= 1e-300:
            continue
        mc = float(np.max(cs)) if cs.size else 0.0
        if not math.isfinite(mc):
            continue
        if mc < 0.0 and mc > -1e-14:
            mc = 0.0
        sc = 2.0 * n * mc / (S * S)
        if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_s = s

    if best_s is None:
        return None

    a_new = a + best_s * delta
    if float(a_new.min()) < -1e-10:
        return None
    a_new = np.maximum(a_new, 0.0)
    snew = float(a_new.sum())
    if snew <= 1e-300:
        return None
    a_new = a_new / snew
    sc_new = _score(a_new)
    if math.isfinite(sc_new) and sc_new < cur_score - 1e-12:
        return a_new, sc_new
    return None


def _newton_step(a, c, target, cur_score):
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return None
    m = 2 * n - 1
    avg = 1.0 / m
    M = float(c.max()) if c.size else 0.0
    if not math.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
        return None
    if target > M:
        target = M
    if target <= avg:
        target = avg * (1.0 + 1e-12)
    red = M - target
    if red <= max(1e-14, 1e-8 * M):
        return None

    d = _box_project(c, target)
    e = c - d
    if not np.any(np.abs(e) > 1e-16 * max(1.0, M)):
        return None

    B = _hankel(a, e)
    r = _autocorr(a)
    sol = _solve_toeplitz(r, -0.5 * B)
    if sol is None:
        return None
    delta = np.asarray(sol, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    delta = delta - float(delta.mean())
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(delta)):
        return None
    return _line_search(a, c, delta, cur_score)


def _gradient_delta(a, c, beta):
    m = float(c.max()) if c.size else 0.0
    if not math.isfinite(m) or m <= 0.0:
        return None
    z = beta * (c - m)
    z = z - float(z.max())
    w = np.exp(z)
    s = float(w.sum())
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return None
    w = w / s
    sv = _hankel(a, w)
    delta = -sv
    delta = delta - float(delta.mean())
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(delta)) or float(np.linalg.norm(delta)) <= 1e-300:
        return None
    return delta


def _active_delta(a, c, frac):
    M = float(c.max()) if c.size else 0.0
    avg = float(c.mean()) if c.size else 0.0
    if not math.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
        return None
    tol = max(1e-15, frac * max(0.0, (M - avg)))
    active = c >= (M - tol)
    if not np.any(active):
        active = np.zeros(c.size, dtype=bool)
        active[int(np.argmax(c))] = True
    w = np.zeros(c.size, dtype=np.float64)
    w[active] = 1.0 / float(np.count_nonzero(active))
    sv = _hankel(a, w)
    delta = -sv
    delta = delta - float(delta.mean())
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(delta)) or float(np.linalg.norm(delta)) <= 1e-300:
        return None
    return delta


def _lp_delta(a, M, time_limit=8.0):
    if linprog is None:
        return None
    n = int(a.size)
    if n < 2 or n > 340:
        return None
    m = 2 * n - 1
    if not math.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
        return None

    A = np.zeros((m, n), dtype=np.float64)
    for j in range(n):
        A[j:j + n, j] = a
    b_ub = np.full(m, M * (1.0 + 1e-9) + 1e-12, dtype=np.float64)
    cobj = -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)

    try:
        res = linprog(
            cobj,
            A_ub=A,
            b_ub=b_ub,
            bounds=[(0.0, None)] * n,
            method="highs",
            options={"presolve": True, "time_limit": float(max(1.0, time_limit))},
        )
    except Exception:
        return None

    if res is not None and getattr(res, "success", False) and getattr(res, "x", None) is not None:
        g = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        g = np.nan_to_num(g, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
        g = np.clip(g, 0.0, None)
        sg = float(g.sum())
        if sg > 1e-14:
            g = g / sg
            delta = g - a
            delta = delta - float(delta.mean())
            if np.all(np.isfinite(delta)) and float(np.linalg.norm(delta)) > 1e-15:
                return delta
    return None


def _make_seeds(n, parent, best_arr, rng):
    n = int(max(1, n))
    seeds = []

    def add(x):
        try:
            x = _clean(x)
            if x.size != n:
                x = _resample(x, n)
            x = _normalize(x)
            if x.size == n and float(x.sum()) > 0.0:
                seeds.append(x)
        except Exception:
            pass

    if parent is not None:
        p = _resample(parent, n)
        add(p)
        for sig in (0.05, 0.15, 0.35):
            add(p * np.exp(sig * rng.standard_normal(n)))

    if best_arr is not None:
        add(_resample(best_arr, n))

    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    xm = x - 0.5

    add(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))

    for cc in (0.3, 0.7, 1.5, 3.0, -0.3, -0.7, -1.5, -3.0):
        add(np.exp(cc * xm))

    for cc in (0.7, 1.5, 3.0):
        add(np.exp(cc * xm) + np.exp(-cc * xm))

    for pp in (0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 4.0):
        add(1.0 + 2.0 * np.abs(2.0 * x - 1.0) ** pp)
        add(1.0 + 2.0 * np.abs(0.5 - x) ** pp)

    for kk in (1, 2, 3):
        for amp in (0.3, 0.6, 0.9):
            add(np.clip(1.0 + amp * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * kk * x), 1e-3, None))
            add(np.clip(1.0 + amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * kk * x), 1e-3, None))

    for alpha in (0.3, 0.7, 1.7, 3.0):
        try:
            add(rng.dirichlet(np.full(n, alpha)))
        except Exception:
            pass

    for sig in (0.3, 0.8, 1.5):
        add(np.exp(sig * rng.standard_normal(n)))

    scored = []
    seen = 0
    for a in seeds:
        if seen >= 60:
            break
        seen += 1
        s = _score(a)
        if math.isfinite(s):
            scored.append((s, a))
    scored.sort(key=lambda t: t[0])
    return [a for _, a in scored[:12]]


def _run_length(n, stop_t, parent, best, rng, base_aim=BASE_AIM):
    now = time.monotonic()
    n = int(n)
    if now >= stop_t - 15 or n < 8:
        return

    seeds = _make_seeds(n, parent, best[1], rng)
    if not seeds:
        seeds = [_normalize(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))]

    max_seeds = 3 if (stop_t - now) > 600 else (2 if (stop_t - now) > 200 else 1)
    chosen = []
    for a in seeds[:max(4, 2 * max_seeds)]:
        ok = True
        for b in chosen:
            if float(np.linalg.norm(a - b)) < 0.03:
                ok = False
                break
        if ok:
            chosen.append(a)
        if len(chosen) >= max_seeds:
            break

    if best[1] is not None and int(best[1].size) == n:
        bb = _normalize(best[1])
        present = False
        for cc in chosen:
            if float(np.linalg.norm(bb - cc)) < 0.02:
                present = True
                break
        if not present:
            chosen.insert(0, bb)
        if len(chosen) > max_seeds:
            chosen = chosen[:max_seeds]

    if not chosen:
        chosen = [_normalize(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))]

    if len(chosen) == 1:
        fracs = [1.0]
    elif len(chosen) == 2:
        fracs = [0.62, 0.38]
    else:
        fracs = [0.52, 0.30, 0.18]

    for i, a0 in enumerate(chosen):
        if time.monotonic() >= stop_t - 12:
            break
        if i > 0 and best[1] is not None and int(best[1].size) == n:
            a0 = _normalize(best[1])

        rem = stop_t - time.monotonic()
        if rem <= 10.0:
            break
        frac = fracs[i] / float(np.sum(fracs[i:]))
        sub_stop = time.monotonic() + max(8.0, rem * frac - 2.0)
        sub_stop = min(sub_stop, stop_t - 5.0)
        if sub_stop - time.monotonic() <= 2.0:
            break
        _polish(a0, sub_stop, best, rng, base_aim, allow_lp=(n <= 340 and linprog is not None))


def _polish(a0, stop_t, best, rng, base_aim=BASE_AIM, allow_lp=True):
    a = _normalize(a0)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n < 8 or time.monotonic() >= stop_t - 4.0:
        return a

    c = np.convolve(a, a)
    sc = _score(a)
    M = float(c.max()) if c.size else 0.0

    local_best_a = a.copy()
    local_best_sc = sc
    local_best_M = M
    if sc <= best[0] + 1e-7:
        _update_best(a, best)

    alpha = 0.16
    fail = 0
    beta = 40.0
    lp_next = -1e18

    while time.monotonic() < stop_t - 1.0:
        a = _normalize(a)
        c = np.convolve(a, a)
        sc = _score(a)
        M = float(c.max()) if c.size else 0.0

        if not math.isfinite(sc):
            break

        if sc < local_best_sc - 1e-14:
            local_best_sc = sc
            local_best_a = a.copy()
            local_best_M = M
            if sc <= best[0] + 1e-7:
                _update_best(a, best)

        avg = 1.0 / float(2 * n - 1)
        aim = base_aim
        if math.isfinite(best[0]):
            aim = min(aim, best[0] - 0.002)
        if aim < 1.0:
            aim = 1.0 + 1e-6
        aim_M = aim / (2.0 * n)

        if M <= aim_M:
            red = 5e-5 * M
        else:
            gap = M - aim_M
            red = alpha * gap
            min_red = max(1e-12, 5e-5 * M)
            if red < min_red:
                red = min_red
            if red > M - avg * 1.0:
                red = M - avg * 1.0
            if red <= 0.0:
                red = min_red

        target = M - red
        step = None

        if target > avg and red > 1e-13:
            step = _newton_step(a, c, target, sc)

        if step is not None:
            a, sc = step
            alpha = min(1.0, alpha * 1.04 + 0.005)
            fail = 0
            beta = max(30.0, beta * 0.95)
            continue

        # Smoothed-max gradient fallback.
        grad_betas = [float(beta), 100.0]
        if fail > 6:
            grad_betas.extend([200.0, 500.0])
        for btry in grad_betas:
            if time.monotonic() >= stop_t - 2.0:
                break
            delta = _gradient_delta(a, c, btry)
            if delta is None:
                continue
            step = _line_search(a, c, delta, sc)
            if step is not None:
                a, sc = step
                beta = float(btry)
                alpha = max(0.10, alpha * 0.9)
                fail = 0
                break

        if step is not None:
            continue

        # Active-set subgradient fallback.
        act_fracs = [0.05, 0.25] if fail < 10 else [0.02, 0.10, 0.45]
        for fr in act_fracs:
            if time.monotonic() >= stop_t - 2.0:
                break
            delta = _active_delta(a, c, fr)
            if delta is None:
                continue
            step = _line_search(a, c, delta, sc)
            if step is not None:
                a, sc = step
                alpha = max(0.10, alpha * 0.85)
                fail = 0
                break

        if step is not None:
            continue

        # Optional LP direction from the previous state-of-art idea.
        if (
            allow_lp
            and linprog is not None
            and n <= 340
            and time.monotonic() >= lp_next
            and (stop_t - time.monotonic()) > 45.0
        ):
            lp_next = time.monotonic() + min(70.0, max(25.0, (stop_t - time.monotonic()) * 0.35))
            tl = max(2.0, min(18.0, (stop_t - time.monotonic() - 35.0) * 0.25))
            delta = _lp_delta(a, M, tl)
            if delta is not None:
                step = _line_search(a, c, delta, sc)
                if step is not None:
                    a, sc = step
                    alpha = max(0.10, alpha * 0.75)
                    fail = 0
                    continue

        fail += 1

        # Perturb / restore to avoid complete stalling.
        if fail == 8 or (fail > 8 and fail % 6 == 0):
            sigma = 0.035 + 0.015 * (fail // 8)
            sigma = min(0.25, sigma)
            moved = False
            for _ in range(5):
                pert = _normalize(a * np.exp(sigma * rng.standard_normal(n)))
                psc = _score(pert)
                if math.isfinite(psc) and psc <= local_best_sc * (1.0 + max(0.01, 0.0005 * fail)):
                    a = pert
                    sc = psc
                    moved = True
                    break
            if not moved:
                a = local_best_a.copy()
                c = np.convolve(a, a)
                sc = local_best_sc
                M = float(c.max()) if c.size else 0.0
            alpha = max(0.08, alpha * 0.55)
            beta = 40.0
            lp_next = time.monotonic() + 30.0
            fail = 0
        elif fail > 30:
            a = local_best_a.copy()
            c = np.convolve(a, a)
            sc = local_best_sc
            M = float(c.max()) if c.size else 0.0
            alpha = 0.08
            beta = 40.0
            fail = 0

    a = _normalize(a)
    sc = _score(a)
    if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < local_best_sc:
        local_best_a = a.copy()
        local_best_sc = sc
        _update_best(a, best)
    elif math.isfinite(sc):
        _update_best(a, best)
    return a


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    try:
        rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))
    except Exception:
        rng = np.random.default_rng(42)

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    try:
        rem_total = max(1.0, float(budget_s))
    except Exception:
        rem_total = 1300.0
    deadline = t0 + rem_total - 20.0

    best = [float("inf"), None]

    parent = None
    try:
        h = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
        if h is not None:
            par = np.asarray(h, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            par = np.nan_to_num(par, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
            par = np.clip(par, 0.0, 1000.0)
            if par.size >= 2 and float(par.sum()) > 1e-12:
                par = _normalize(par)
                parent = par
                _update_best(par, best)
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    if parent is None:
        parent = _normalize(np.full(192, 1.0, dtype=np.float64))
        _update_best(parent, best)

    if best[1] is None:
        fin = np.array([1.0, 1.0, 1.0], dtype=np.float64)
        _update_best(fin, best)
        fin = best[1] if best[1] is not None else fin
        _update_best(fin, best)

    parent_n = int(parent.size) if parent is not None else 192
    if parent_n < 16:
        parent_n = 192

    task_map = {}

    def add_task(n, w):
        n = int(n)
        if 24 <= n <= 512:
            if n not in task_map:
                task_map[n] = float(w)
            else:
                task_map[n] = max(task_map[n], float(w))

    add_task(parent_n, 2.6)
    add_task(192, 2.1)
    add_task(256, 3.0)
    add_task(320, 2.0)
    if (deadline - t0) > 700.0:
        add_task(384, 1.5)
    if (deadline - t0) > 1050.0:
        add_task(448, 1.0)

    if not task_map:
        add_task(192, 1.0)

    tasks = sorted(task_map.items(), key=lambda kv: (kv[0] != parent_n, -kv[1]))
    remaining = tasks[:]

    while remaining and time.monotonic() < deadline - 30.0:
        n, w = remaining[0]
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem < 25.0:
            break
        wsum = sum(ww for _, ww in remaining)
        frac = w / wsum
        alloc = rem * frac
        if len(remaining) > 1:
            alloc = max(30.0, min(520.0, alloc))
        stop_t = time.monotonic() + alloc
        stop_t = min(stop_t, deadline - 25.0)
        if stop_t - time.monotonic() < 10.0:
            stop_t = time.monotonic() + min(rem, 20.0)
        _run_length(n, stop_t, parent, best, rng, BASE_AIM)
        remaining.pop(0)

    # Final validation and safe return.
    fin = best[1] if best[1] is not None else parent
    try:
        fin = _normalize(fin)
        if fin.size < 1:
            fin = np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
        v = evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in fin])
        if not math.isfinite(v):
            fin = np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
            v = evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in fin])
            if not math.isfinite(v):
                fin = np.array([1.0, 1.0], dtype=np.float64)
                v = evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in fin])
                if not math.isfinite(v):
                    fin = np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    except Exception:
        fin = np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)

    if fin is None or fin.size == 0:
        fin = np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)

    return [float(x) for x in fin]
```
